Concept: List Comprehension
Definition: Creates lists using concise syntax.
Key Points:
- Commonly used in Python development.
- Frequently asked in interviews.
- Important for writing clean and efficient Python code.
Example:
[x*x for x in range(5)]
Related Concepts:
Variables, Functions, OOP, Modules, Packages, Data Structures
